Structural cue: initial oath begins with bodies in motion and breath, then 100:3 names a morning raid and 100:5 an entry into a جمع. Role: عدو supplies speed and hostile crossing; ضبح makes the action audible and animal-bodied. Abductive move: the panting is not neutral locomotion but the sound of a force crossing into contested space. Reading change: the ayah reads as an embodied threshold breach, not simply a description of fast runners.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000901/B003","root_000901/B004","root_000901/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0d0d5286359ea9f32448","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:1:2\",\"root_000901\",[\"B003\",\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Soluk sesi, sonraki kıvılcım ve tozla birlikte sürtünmeden doğan ısı, kararma ve külün ilk maddi evresi gibi okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000782/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0f84a08dd2f534c92eb5","text":"100:8 surprisingly returned the opening run inside the human: ش د د B003 and حب as clinging made the panting motion a figure for inward compulsion. 100:5 also clarified the spatial path: 100:1 begins at speed/edge, while 100:5 completes the maneuver in the center.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1b1ad0830ef30d755491","text":"100:6 corrects the tempting transgression reading. The explicit negative diagnosis, `ك ن د B002`, attaches to the human there, not to the runners here, so `ع د و B001` must remain an exploratory boundary-crossing overlay rather than a settled moral identity. By contrast, 100:2 and 100:4 strengthen the material surprise: striking fire and raised particulate make the scorching and ash branches of `ض ب ح` genuinely functional in the sequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000901/B003","root_000901/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1be2dc6b62e5bbf41bbc","text":"3. **Breath as the first material stage of scorching.** Alongside its sound sense, `ض ب ح B003` carries scorching of wood or striker-stones, `B004` blackening, and `B005` ash. Those dormant images are activated when 100:2 produces hidden fire by striking and 100:4 produces an airborne particulate cloud. I infer, rather than receive from the packet, a single frictional chain from exertion through ignition to dark residue. `ضَبْحًا` can then register not only panting but the incipient heat and residue generated by the charge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000839/B004","root_000993/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1cb7089c14f51032f692","text":"2. **A coexisting boundary-crossing reading.** `ع د و B001` names overstepping a bound into aggression, and `ع د و B004` names passing beyond. The later morning-raid branch at `ص ب ح B004` in 100:3 and entry into the middle of a gathered body in 100:5 turn linear running into crossed perimeter and breached center. The packet does not label the crossing morally; the arrow from spatial breach to possible transgression is my inference. The runners can therefore remain literal runners while also reading as agents whose motion exceeds a boundary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000993/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4e12a94a50f6f3760a94","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:1:1\",\"root_000993\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Hareket, kenar boyunca ilerleyip topluluğun merkezine ulaşan mekânsal bir manevranın başlangıcıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000901/B001","root_000993/B006","root_000993/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5d4ab482e347a9adf8ff","text":"2. **Contagious condition of drive.** Evidence: ع د و B006 contagion/transmission and B007 hindrances; ض ب ح B001 repeated breath/cry; later ح ب ب B002 clinging love and ش د د B002 intensity. Structural cue: the opening motion becomes moral diagnosis in 100:6-8. Role: \"running\" becomes a transmissible pattern: driven desire passes from scene to human interior. Abductive move: the packet invites analogy between external gallop and internal compulsion. Reading change: the panting runners prefigure a human soul infected by severe attachment, making 100:1 a motor-image for later appetite.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000901/B001","root_000993/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6c4183fd9bc6f174a648","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:1:1\",\"root_000993\",[\"B002\"]],[\"100:1:2\",\"root_000901\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Soluk soluğa koşu, kıvılcım, baskın, toz ve merkeze giriş zincirini harekete geçirir; duyulan soluk da bu hareketin bedensel maliyetini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000993/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7c2ec00028d11b176bad","text":"1. **Exertion as the propulsion of a causal sequence.** `ع د و B002` supplies running and hastening, while `ض ب ح B001` supplies the audible breath or mouth-sound of horses in motion. The participial runner plus the accusative breath-image is immediately continued by sparks, dawn arrival, raised dust, and penetration in 100:2-5. Running is the mover and breath is its measurable cost. The packet gives the ordered effects; I infer that exertive motion enables the impacts that follow. The fixed ayah thus reads as the engine of the sequence, not as a static picture of speed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000993/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_93dc5416fc7b842a2b5c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:1:1\",\"root_000993\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Dıştaki koşu, insanın mala şiddetli bağlılığında içe taşınan bulaşıcı bir sürüklenme kalıbını önceden gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000993/B001","root_000993/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_aa5739787c64c9795642","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:1:1\",\"root_000993\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Koşu, yalnız hız değil, düşmanlık taşıyan bir sınır aşımı olarak da belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000901/B002","root_000993/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fbfa7e8b0f55f28fa6c2","text":"3. **Along-edge approach.** Evidence: ع د و B009 side/bank/along-edge stretch, B010 hard uneven ground; ض ب ح B002 light or stretched running; later و س ط B003 entering the middle. Structural cue: the sequence runs from edge movement to center penetration. Role: عدو marks movement along a flank or boundary; وسط later reverses it by entry into the middle. Abductive move: a successful incursion first traces the edge before cutting into the center. Reading change: the ayah becomes the beginning of a spatial maneuver, not merely a generic chase.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
